   65:   | {
   66:       ok: true;
   67:       startedAt: string;
   68:       endedAt: string;
   69:       durationMinutes: number;
   70:     }
   71:   | {
   72:       ok: false;
   73:       error: string;
   74:     };
   75: 
   76: 
   77: function asRecord(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
   78:   if (typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value)) {
   79:     return value as Record<string, unknown>;
   80:   }
   81: 
   82:   return {};
   83: }

  203: async function getExistingImpactEventsCount(eventId: string) {
  204:   const { count, error } = await supabase
  205:     .from("impact_events")
  206:     .select("id", {
  207:       count: "exact",

  228:       : ACTIVITY_RECORDING_DISABLED_MESSAGE,

  241: export async function POST(request: Request) {
  242:   if (!ACTIVITY_RECORDING_ENABLED) {
  243:     return NextResponse.json(
  244:       {
  245:         ok: false,
  246:         error: ACTIVITY_RECORDING_DISABLED_MESSAGE,
  247:       },
  248:       { status: 503 }
  249:     );
  250:   }
  251: 
  252:   const { appUser, errorResponse } = await getActivityUserContext();
  253: 
  254:   if (errorResponse) {
  255:     return errorResponse;
  256:   }
  257: 
  258:   if (!appUser) {
  259:     return NextResponse.json(
  260:       {
  261:         ok: false,
  262:         error: "User context not found",
  263:       },
  264:       { status: 500 }
  265:     );
  266:   }
  267: 
  268:   let body: ActivityCompleteBody;
  269: 
  270:   try {
  271:     body = (await request.json()) as ActivityCompleteBody;
  272:   } catch {
  273:     return NextResponse.json(
  274:       {
  275:         ok: false,
  276:         error: "Invalid JSON body",
  277:       },
  278:       { status: 400 }
  279:     );
  280:   }
  281: 
  282:   const eventId = asString(body.eventId);
  283: 
  284:   if (!eventId) {
  285:     return NextResponse.json(
  286:       {
  287:         ok: false,
  288:         error: "eventId is required.",
  289:       },
  290:       { status: 400 }
  291:     );
  292:   }
  293: 
  294:   const { data: eventData, error: eventError } = await supabase
  295:     .from("activity_events")
  296:     .select("*")
  297:     .eq("id", eventId)
  298:     .eq("user_id", appUser.id)
  299:     .maybeSingle();
  300: 
  301:   if (eventError) {
  302:     return NextResponse.json(
  303:       {
  304:         ok: false,
  305:         error: eventError.message,
  306:       },
  307:       { status: 500 }
  308:     );
  309:   }
  310: 
  311:   if (!eventData) {
  312:     return NextResponse.json(
  313:       {
  314:         ok: false,
  315:         error: "Activity event not found or access denied.",
  316:       },
  317:       { status: 404 }
  318:     );
  319:   }

  272: function readStringField(
  273:   record: Record<string, unknown>,
  274:   keys: string[]
  275: ): string | null {
  276:   for (const key of keys) {
  277:     const value = record[key];
  278: 
  279:     if (typeof value === "string" && value.trim().length > 0) {
  280:       return value;
  281:     }
  282:   }
  283: 
  284:   return null;
  285: }

------------------------------------------------------------
FUNCTION: readNumberField
FILE: src/app/api/activity/debug/free-text-value-object-test/route.ts
REQUIRED: False
RANGE: 287-308
------------------------------------------------------------
  287: function readNumberField(
  288:   record: Record<string, unknown>,
  289:   keys: string[]
  290: ): number | null {
  291:   for (const key of keys) {
  292:     const value = record[key];
  293: 
  294:     if (typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value)) {
  295:       return value;
  296:     }
  297: 
  298:     if (typeof value === "string" && value.trim().length > 0) {
  299:       const parsed = Number(value);
  300: 
  301:       if (Number.isFinite(parsed)) {
  302:         return parsed;
  303:       }
  304:     }
  305:   }
  306: 
  307:   return null;
  308: }

  422: function buildAdditionalCategoryLinksForBridge(params: {
  423:   categoryDerivationEnabled: boolean;
  424:   categoryDerivationDryRun: boolean;
  425:   activityEventId: string;
  426:   derivationRunId: string | null;
  427:   categoryDerivationResult: unknown;
  428: }): AdditionalValueObjectCategoryLink[] | undefined {
  429:   const {
  430:     categoryDerivationEnabled,
  431:     categoryDerivationDryRun,
  432:     activityEventId,
  433:     derivationRunId,
  434:     categoryDerivationResult,
  435:   } = params;
  436: 
  437:   if (!categoryDerivationEnabled || categoryDerivationDryRun) {
  438:     return undefined;
  439:   }
  440: 
  441:   const resolvedCandidates =
  442:     collectPossibleResolvedCandidates(categoryDerivationResult);
  443:   const derivationRowsBySlug = buildDerivationRowBySlug(
  444:     collectPossibleDerivationRows(categoryDerivationResult)
  445:   );
  446: 
  447:   const allowedResolutionStatuses = new Set([
  448:     "resolved_existing",
  449:     "created_suggested",
  450:     "created_active",
  451:   ]);
  452: 
  453:   const links: AdditionalValueObjectCategoryLink[] = [];
  454: 
  455:   for (const candidate of resolvedCandidates) {
  456:     const categoryId = readStringField(candidate, [
  457:       "categoryId",
  458:       "category_id",
  459:       "resolvedCategoryId",
  460:       "resolved_category_id",
  461:     ]);
  462: 
  463:     if (!isUuidLike(categoryId)) {
  464:       continue;
  465:     }
  466: 
  467:     const resolutionStatus =
  468:       readStringField(candidate, ["resolutionStatus", "resolution_status"]) ??
  469:       "resolved_existing";
  470: 
  471:     if (!allowedResolutionStatuses.has(resolutionStatus)) {
  472:       continue;
  473:     }
  474: 
  475:     const candidateSlug =
  476:       readStringField(candidate, [
  477:         "candidateSlug",
  478:         "candidate_slug",
  479:         "categorySlug",
  480:         "category_slug",
  481:         "slug",
  482:       ]) ?? categoryId;
  483: 
  484:     const derivationRow = derivationRowsBySlug.get(candidateSlug) ?? null;
  485: 
  486:     const activityCategoryDerivationId = derivationRow
  487:       ? readStringField(derivationRow, ["id", "activityCategoryDerivationId"])
  488:       : null;
  489: 
  490:     links.push({
  491:       categoryId,
  492:       categoryTable: "contextual_categories",
  493:       categoryRole: "semantic_component",
  494:       source: "rule",
  495:       confidence:
  496:         readNumberField(candidate, ["confidence", "score"]) ??
  497:         readNumberField(derivationRow ?? {}, ["confidence", "score"]),
  498:       derivationRunId,
  499:       activityCategoryDerivationId,
  500:       activityEventId,
  501:       candidateSlug,
  502:       candidateTitle: readStringField(candidate, [
  503:         "candidateTitle",
  504:         "candidate_title",
  505:         "title",
  506:         "label",
  507:         "name",
  508:       ]),
  509:       semanticLayer: readStringField(candidate, [
  510:         "semanticLayer",
  511:         "semantic_layer",
  512:       ]),
  513:       categoryType: readStringField(candidate, ["categoryType", "category_type"]),
  514:       resolutionStatus,
  515:       metadata: {
  516:         sourceLayer: "category_derivation",
  517:         sourceRoute: "/api/activity/debug/free-text-value-object-test",
  518:         p4Step: "P4.10.0-C8-P3-B5-B3",
  519:       },
  520:     });
  521:   }
  522: 
  523:   return links.length > 0 ? links : undefined;
  524: }

   88: function asString(value: unknown): string | null {
   89:   if (typeof value !== "string") {
   90:     return null;
   91:   }
   92: 
   93:   const trimmed = value.trim();
   94:   return trimmed.length > 0 ? trimmed : null;
   95: }
   96: 
   97: function asNumber(value: unknown): number | null {
   98:   if (typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value)) {
   99:     return value;
  100:   }
  101: 
  102:   if (typeof value === "string") {
  103:     const normalized = value.trim().replace(",", ".");
  104:     const parsed = Number.parseFloat(normalized);
  105: 
  106:     if (Number.isFinite(parsed)) {
  107:       return parsed;
  108:     }
  109:   }
  110: 
  111:   return null;
  112: }
  113: 
  114: function asBoolean(value: unknown): boolean | null {
  115:   if (typeof value === "boolean") {
  116:     return value;
  117:   }
  118: 
  119:   if (typeof value !== "string") {
  120:     return null;
  121:   }
  122: 
  123:   const normalized = value.trim().toLowerCase();
  124: 
  125:   if (["true", "1", "yes", "y", "on"].includes(normalized)) {
  126:     return true;
  127:   }
  128: 
  129:   if (["false", "0", "no", "n", "off"].includes(normalized)) {

  400: function buildDerivationRowBySlug(
  401:   rows: Record<string, unknown>[]
  402: ): Map<string, Record<string, unknown>> {
  403:   const result = new Map<string, Record<string, unknown>>();
  404: 
  405:   for (const row of rows) {
  406:     const slug = readStringField(row, [
  407:       "candidateSlug",
  408:       "candidate_slug",
  409:       "categorySlug",
  410:       "category_slug",
  411:       "slug",
  412:     ]);
  413: 
  414:     if (slug) {
  415:       result.set(slug, row);
  416:     }
  417:   }
  418: 
  419:   return result;
  420: }

  422: function buildAdditionalCategoryLinksForBridge(params: {
  423:   categoryDerivationEnabled: boolean;
  424:   categoryDerivationDryRun: boolean;
  425:   activityEventId: string;
  426:   derivationRunId: string | null;
  427:   categoryDerivationResult: unknown;
  428: }): AdditionalValueObjectCategoryLink[] | undefined {
  429:   const {
  430:     categoryDerivationEnabled,

  433:     derivationRunId,
  434:     categoryDerivationResult,
  435:   } = params;
  436: 
  437:   if (!categoryDerivationEnabled || categoryDerivationDryRun) {
  438:     return undefined;
  439:   }
  440: 
  441:   const resolvedCandidates =
  442:     collectPossibleResolvedCandidates(categoryDerivationResult);
  443:   const derivationRowsBySlug = buildDerivationRowBySlug(
  444:     collectPossibleDerivationRows(categoryDerivationResult)
  445:   );
  446: 
  447:   const allowedResolutionStatuses = new Set([
  448:     "resolved_existing",
  449:     "created_suggested",
  450:     "created_active",
  451:   ]);
  452: 
  453:   const links: AdditionalValueObjectCategoryLink[] = [];
  454: 
  455:   for (const candidate of resolvedCandidates) {
  456:     const categoryId = readStringField(candidate, [
  457:       "categoryId",
  458:       "category_id",
  459:       "resolvedCategoryId",
  460:       "resolved_category_id",
  461:     ]);
  462: 
  463:     if (!isUuidLike(categoryId)) {
  464:       continue;
  465:     }
  466: 
  467:     const resolutionStatus =
  468:       readStringField(candidate, ["resolutionStatus", "resolution_status"]) ??
  469:       "resolved_existing";
  470: 
  471:     if (!allowedResolutionStatuses.has(resolutionStatus)) {
  472:       continue;
  473:     }
  474: 
  475:     const candidateSlug =
  476:       readStringField(candidate, [
  477:         "candidateSlug",
  478:         "candidate_slug",
  479:         "categorySlug",
  480:         "category_slug",
  481:         "slug",
  482:       ]) ?? categoryId;
  483: 
  484:     const derivationRow = derivationRowsBySlug.get(candidateSlug) ?? null;
  485: 
  486:     const activityCategoryDerivationId = derivationRow
  487:       ? readStringField(derivationRow, ["id", "activityCategoryDerivationId"])
  488:       : null;
  489: 
  490:     links.push({
  491:       categoryId,
  492:       categoryTable: "contextual_categories",
  493:       categoryRole: "semantic_component",
  494:       source: "rule",
  495:       confidence:
  496:         readNumberField(candidate, ["confidence", "score"]) ??
  497:         readNumberField(derivationRow ?? {}, ["confidence", "score"]),
  498:       derivationRunId,
  499:       activityCategoryDerivationId,
  500:       activityEventId,
  501:       candidateSlug,
  502:       candidateTitle: readStringField(candidate, [
  503:         "candidateTitle",
  504:         "candidate_title",
  505:         "title",
  506:         "label",
  507:         "name",
  508:       ]),
  509:       semanticLayer: readStringField(candidate, [
  510:         "semanticLayer",
  511:         "semantic_layer",
  512:       ]),
  513:       categoryType: readStringField(candidate, ["categoryType", "category_type"]),
  514:       resolutionStatus,
  515:       metadata: {
  516:         sourceLayer: "category_derivation",
  517:         sourceRoute: "/api/activity/debug/free-text-value-object-test",
  518:         p4Step: "P4.10.0-C8-P3-B5-B3",
  519:       },
  520:     });
  521:   }
  522: 
  523:   return links.length > 0 ? links : undefined;
  524: }

  251: function normalizeV42ProjectionSource(source: BridgeSource): V42ProjectionSource {
  252:   if (source === "manual") {
  253:     return "manual";
  254:   }
  255: 
  256:   if (source === "ai_draft") {
  257:     return "ai";
  258:   }
  259: 
  260:   if (source === "system") {
  261:     return "system_seed";
  262:   }
  263: 
  264:   /*
  265:    * The v4.2 projection/category tables currently allow:
  266:    * rule | ai | manual | system_seed | migration
  267:    *
  268:    * Bridge-specific sources such as api/correction/commercial are kept in metadata,
  269:    * while the table-level source remains rule-compatible.
  270:    */
  271:   return "rule";
  272: }

 1036: async function createAdditionalValueObjectCategoryLinks(params: {
 1037:   supabase: SupabaseClient;
 1038:   eventId: string;
 1039:   valueObjectId: string;
 1040:   activityEventValueObjectLinkId: string | null;
 1041:   processorName: string;
 1042:   additionalCategoryLinks: AdditionalValueObjectCategoryLink[] | null | undefined;
 1043: }): Promise<{
 1044:   created: Array<{
 1045:     valueObjectCategoryLinkId: string | null;
 1046:     categoryId: string;
 1047:     candidateSlug: string;
 1048:     errorMessage: string | null;
 1049:   }>;
 1050:   errors: string[];
 1051: }> {
 1052:   const {
 1053:     supabase,
 1054:     eventId,
 1055:     valueObjectId,
 1056:     activityEventValueObjectLinkId,
 1057:     processorName,
 1058:     additionalCategoryLinks,
 1059:   } = params;
 1060: 
 1061:   const created: Array<{
 1062:     valueObjectCategoryLinkId: string | null;
 1063:     categoryId: string;
 1064:     candidateSlug: string;
 1065:     errorMessage: string | null;
 1066:   }> = [];
 1067:   const errors: string[] = [];
 1068: 
 1069:   if (!additionalCategoryLinks || additionalCategoryLinks.length === 0) {
 1070:     return {
 1071:       created,
 1072:       errors,
 1073:     };
 1074:   }
 1075: 
 1076:   for (const item of additionalCategoryLinks) {
 1077:     if (!isUuid(item.categoryId)) {
 1078:       errors.push(
 1079:         `Skipped additional category link with invalid categoryId for candidate ${item.candidateSlug}.`
 1080:       );
 1081:       continue;
 1082:     }
 1083: 
 1084:     const categoryTable = item.categoryTable ?? "contextual_categories";
 1085: 
 1086:     if (categoryTable !== "contextual_categories") {
 1087:       errors.push(
 1088:         `Skipped additional category link with unsupported categoryTable ${categoryTable} for candidate ${item.candidateSlug}.`
 1089:       );
 1090:       continue;
 1091:     }
 1092: 
 1093:     const categoryRole: ValueObjectCategoryRole =

 1182: export async function processValueObjectBridgeForActivityEvent(
 1183:   input: ProcessValueObjectBridgeInput
 1184: ): Promise<ProcessValueObjectBridgeResult> {
 1185:   const {
 1186:     supabase,
 1187:     eventId,
 1188:     mappings,
 1189:     source,
 1190:     allowNonCompletedEvent = false,
 1191:     processorName = "value_object_bridge_p4_7",
 1192:     additionalCategoryLinks,
 1193:   } = input;
 1194: 
 1195:   const result: ProcessValueObjectBridgeResult = {
 1196:     ok: false,
 1197:     skipped: false,
 1198:     skipReason: null,
 1199:     eventId,
 1200:     eventStatus: null,
 1201:     mappingsRequested: mappings.length,
 1202:     created: [],
 1203:     errors: [],
 1204:   };
 1205: 
 1206:   if (mappings.length === 0) {
 1207:     result.ok = true;
 1208:     result.skipped = true;
 1209:     result.skipReason = "no_mappings";
 1210:     return result;
 1211:   }
 1212: 
 1213:   const { event, errorMessage } = await readActivityEvent(supabase, eventId);
 1214: 
 1215:   if (errorMessage) {
 1216:     result.errors.push(errorMessage);
 1217:     return result;
 1218:   }
 1219: 
 1220:   if (!event) {
 1221:     result.errors.push("Activity event not found.");
 1222:     return result;
 1223:   }
 1224: 
 1225:   result.eventStatus = event.status;
 1226: 
 1227:   if (!allowNonCompletedEvent && event.status !== "completed") {
 1228:     result.ok = true;
 1229:     result.skipped = true;
 1230:     result.skipReason = `event_status_${event.status}_not_completed`;
 1231:     return result;
 1232:   }
 1233: 
 1234:   for (const mapping of mappings) {
 1235:     const mappingSource = normalizeSource(mapping.source ?? source);
 1236:     const confidence = clamp01(mapping.confidence, 1);
 1237:     const weight = clamp01(mapping.weight, 1);
 1238:     const deltaDirection = normalizeDeltaDirection(mapping.deltaDirection);
 1239:     const relationType = normalizeRelationType(mapping.relationType);
 1240:     const aggregateDate = mapping.aggregateDate ?? getDateFromEvent(event);
 1241:     const aggregateType = mapping.aggregateType ?? "value_object";
 1242:     const aggregateKey = mapping.aggregateKey ?? mapping.valueObjectId;
 1243:     const signedDelta = getSignedNumericDelta(
 1244:       mapping.deltaValueNumeric ?? null,
 1245:       deltaDirection
 1246:     );
 1247: 
 1248:     const createdItem: ValueObjectBridgeCreatedItem = {
 1249:       valueObjectId: mapping.valueObjectId,
 1250:       valueObjectInstanceId: null,
 1251:       linkId: null,
 1252:       stateDeltaId: null,
 1253:       aggregateId: null,
 1254:       snapshotId: null,
 1255:       activityEventValueObjectLinkId: null,
 1256:       usageAggregateId: null,
 1257:       v42ProjectionError: null,
 1258:       valueObjectCategoryLinkId: null,
 1259:       valueObjectCategoryLinkError: null,
 1260:       additionalValueObjectCategoryLinks: [],
 1261:       additionalValueObjectCategoryLinkErrors: [],
 1262:       skipped: false,
 1263:       skipReason: null,
 1264:     };
 1265: 
 1266:     if (
 1267:       mapping.deltaValueNumeric === null &&
 1268:       mapping.deltaValueText === null &&
 1269:       typeof mapping.deltaValueNumeric !== "number" &&
 1270:       typeof mapping.deltaValueText !== "string"
 1271:     ) {
 1272:       createdItem.skipped = true;
 1273:       createdItem.skipReason = "missing_delta_value";
 1274:       result.created.push(createdItem);
 1275:       continue;
 1276:     }
 1277: 
 1278:     const existingStateDelta = await readExistingStateDeltaForMapping(
 1279:       supabase,
 1280:       event.id,
 1281:       mapping.valueObjectId,
 1282:       mapping.metricKey
 1283:     );
 1284: 
 1285:     if (existingStateDelta.errorMessage) {
 1286:       createdItem.skipped = true;
 1287:       createdItem.skipReason = existingStateDelta.errorMessage;
 1288:       result.created.push(createdItem);
 1289:       continue;
 1290:     }
 1291: 
 1292:     if (existingStateDelta.stateDeltaId) {
 1293:       createdItem.valueObjectInstanceId =
 1294:         existingStateDelta.valueObjectInstanceId;
 1295:       createdItem.stateDeltaId = existingStateDelta.stateDeltaId;
 1296:       createdItem.skipped = true;
 1297:       createdItem.skipReason = "already_processed_event_value_object_metric";
 1298:       result.created.push(createdItem);
 1299:       continue;
 1300:     }
 1301: 
 1302:     const ownerContext = await readValueObjectOwnerContext(

  256: export async function resolveCategoryCandidates(
  257:   supabase: CategoryResolverSupabaseClient,
  258:   candidates: CategoryCandidate[],
  259:   options: CategoryResolverOptions = {},
  260: ): Promise<CategoryResolutionResult> {
  261:   const createPolicy = options.createPolicy ?? "suggested_only";
  262:   const resolved: ResolvedCategoryCandidate[] = [];
  263:   const warnings: string[] = [];
  264:   const errors: string[] = [];
  265: 
  266:   let createdCount = 0;
  267:   let reusedCount = 0;
  268:   let unresolvedCount = 0;
  269: 
  270:   const defaultContextResult =
  271:     await findDefaultCategoryDerivationContextId(supabase);
  272: 
  273:   const defaultContextId = defaultContextResult.contextId;
  274: 
  275:   if (defaultContextResult.error) {
  276:     warnings.push(
  277:       `Default category derivation context lookup failed: ${defaultContextResult.error}`,
  278:     );
  279:   }
  280: 
  281:   if (!defaultContextId && createPolicy !== "never") {
  282:     warnings.push(
  283:       `Default category derivation context not found: ${DEFAULT_CATEGORY_DERIVATION_CONTEXT_CODE}`,
  284:     );
  285:   }
  286: 
  287:   for (const candidate of candidates) {
  288:     const normalizedSlug = normalizeCategoryCandidateSlug(candidate.slug);
  289: 
  290:     if (normalizedSlug.length === 0) {
  291:       unresolvedCount += 1;
  292:       warnings.push(`Candidate slug could not be normalized: ${candidate.slug}`);
  293:       resolved.push({
  294:         ...candidate,
  295:         categoryId: null,
  296:         resolutionStatus: "unresolved",
  297:         metadata: {
  298:           ...(candidate.metadata ?? {}),
  299:           normalizedSlug,
  300:           resolverWarning: "empty_normalized_slug",
  301:         },
  302:       });
  303:       continue;
  304:     }
  305: 
  306:     const semanticLayer = candidate.semanticLayer
  307:       ? String(candidate.semanticLayer)
  308:       : undefined;
  309:     const existing = await findExistingCategory(
  310:       supabase,
  311:       normalizedSlug,
  312:       semanticLayer,
  313:       defaultContextId,
  314:     );
  315: 
  316:     if (existing.error) {
  317:       errors.push(`Lookup failed for ${normalizedSlug}: ${existing.error}`);
  318:     }
  319: 
  320:     if (existing.row?.id) {
  321:       reusedCount += 1;
  322:       resolved.push({
  323:         ...candidate,
  324:         slug: normalizedSlug,
  325:         title:
  326:           candidate.title ??
  327:           existing.row.title ??
  328:           existing.row.name ??
  329:           normalizeTitle(candidate),
  330:         categoryId: existing.row.id,
  331:         resolutionStatus: "resolved_existing",
  332:         metadata: {
  333:           ...(candidate.metadata ?? {}),
  334:           normalizedSlug,
  335:           existingStatus: existing.row.status ?? null,
  336:         },
  337:       });
  338:       continue;
  339:     }
  340: 
  341:     if (!shouldCreateCategory(candidate, createPolicy) || options.dryRun) {
  342:       unresolvedCount += 1;
  343:       resolved.push({
  344:         ...candidate,
  345:         slug: normalizedSlug,
  346:         categoryId: null,
  347:         resolutionStatus: "unresolved",
  348:         metadata: {
  349:           ...(candidate.metadata ?? {}),
  350:           normalizedSlug,
  351:           dryRun: options.dryRun ?? false,
  352:           createPolicy,
  353:         },
  354:       });
  355:       continue;
  356:     }
  357: 
  358:     if (!defaultContextId) {
  359:       unresolvedCount += 1;
  360:       warnings.push(
  361:         `Cannot create contextual category without default context: ${normalizedSlug}`,
  362:       );
  363:       resolved.push({
  364:         ...candidate,
  365:         categoryId: null,
  366:         resolutionStatus: "unresolved",

   81: function normalizeRunStatus(
   82:   derivationResult: CategoryDerivationResult,
   83: ): CategoryDerivationRunStatus {
   84:   if (derivationResult.errors.length > 0 || derivationResult.ok === false) {
   85:     return "failed";
   86:   }
   87:

   89:     return "completed_with_warnings";
   90:   }

   95: function resolvedOrUnresolvedCandidates(
   96:   derivationResult: CategoryDerivationResult,
   97:   resolvedCandidates?: ResolvedCategoryCandidate[],

  100:     return resolvedCandidates;
  101:   }

  104:     ...candidate,
  105:     categoryId: null,
  106:     resolutionStatus: "unresolved",
  107:   }));
  108: }

  111:   return candidates.filter((candidate) => Boolean(candidate.categoryId)).length;
  112: }
  113:

  175: function buildDerivationInsert(
  176:   activityEventId: string,
  177:   derivationRunId: string,
  178:   candidate: ResolvedCategoryCandidate,
  179: ): ActivityCategoryDerivationInsert {
  180:   return {
  181:     activity_event_id: activityEventId,
  182:     derivation_run_id: derivationRunId,
  183:     category_id: candidate.categoryId ?? null,
  184:     candidate_slug: candidate.slug,
  185:     candidate_title: candidate.title ?? null,
  186:     semantic_layer: candidate.semanticLayer
  187:       ? String(candidate.semanticLayer)
  188:       : null,
  189:     category_type: candidate.categoryType ?? null,
  190:     source: candidateSource(candidate),
  191:     confidence: candidate.confidence ?? null,
  192:     is_required: candidate.isRequired ?? false,
  193:     is_confirmed: candidate.isConfirmed ?? false,
  194:     needs_user_review: candidate.needsUserReview ?? false,
  195:     is_rejected: false,
  196:     metadata_json: toJsonRecord({
  197:       ...(candidate.metadata ?? {}),
  198:       resolutionStatus: candidate.resolutionStatus,
  199:       categoryId: candidate.categoryId ?? null,
  200:     }),
  201:   };
  202: }

  204: async function insertDerivationRun(
  205:   supabase: CategoryDerivationPersistenceSupabaseClient,
  206:   payload: CategoryDerivationRunInsert,
  207: ): Promise<{
  208:   row: CategoryDerivationRunRow | null;
  209:   error: string | null;
  210: }> {
  211:   const result = await supabase
  212:     .from<CategoryDerivationRunRow>("category_derivation_runs")
  213:     .insert(payload as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)
  214:     .select("*")
  215:     .maybeSingle();
  216: 
  217:   return {
  218:     row: result.data ?? null,
  219:     error: errorMessage(result.error),
  220:   };
  221: }

  227:   row: ActivityCategoryDerivationRow | null;
  228:   error: string | null;

  231:     .from<ActivityCategoryDerivationRow>("activity_category_derivations")
  232:     .insert(payload as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)
  233:     .select("*")
  234:     .maybeSingle();
  235: 
  236:   return {
  237:     row: result.data ?? null,
  238:     error: errorMessage(result.error),
  239:   };

  241: 
  242: export async function persistCategoryDerivations(
  243:   supabase: CategoryDerivationPersistenceSupabaseClient,
  244:   params: PersistCategoryDerivationsParams,
  245: ): Promise<PersistCategoryDerivationsResult> {
  246:   const warnings: string[] = [];
  247:   const errors: string[] = [];
  248: 
  249:   if (!params.activityEventId || params.activityEventId.trim().length === 0) {
  250:     return {
  251:       ok: false,
  252:       derivationRunId: null,
  253:       derivationRowsCreated: 0,
  254:       candidateCount: params.derivationResult.candidates.length,
  255:       resolvedCandidateCount: 0,
  256:       unresolvedCandidateCount: params.derivationResult.candidates.length,
  257:       warnings,
  258:       errors: ["activityEventId is required."],
  259:     };
  260:   }
  261: 
  262:   const candidates = resolvedOrUnresolvedCandidates(
  263:     params.derivationResult,
  264:     params.resolvedCandidates,
  265:   );
  266:   const runInsert = buildRunInsert(params, candidates);
  267:   const runResult = await insertDerivationRun(supabase, runInsert);
  268: 
  269:   if (runResult.error || !runResult.row?.id) {
  270:     return {
  271:       ok: false,
  272:       derivationRunId: null,
  273:       derivationRowsCreated: 0,
  274:       candidateCount: candidates.length,
  275:       resolvedCandidateCount: countResolved(candidates),
  276:       unresolvedCandidateCount: countUnresolved(candidates),
  277:       warnings,
  278:       errors: [
  279:         `Failed to insert category_derivation_runs: ${
  280:           runResult.error ?? "no row returned"
  281:         }`,
  282:       ],
  283:       runInsert,
  284:     };
  285:   }
  286: 
  287:   const derivationRunId = runResult.row.id;
  288:   const derivationInserts = candidates.map((candidate) =>
  289:     buildDerivationInsert(params.activityEventId, derivationRunId, candidate),
  290:   );
  291: 
  292:   let derivationRowsCreated = 0;
  293: 
  294:   for (const insertPayload of derivationInserts) {
  295:     const insertResult = await insertActivityCategoryDerivation(
  296:       supabase,
  297:       insertPayload,
  298:     );
  299: 
  300:     if (insertResult.error || !insertResult.row?.id) {
  301:       errors.push(
  302:         `Failed to insert activity_category_derivations for ${insertPayload.candidate_slug}: ${
  303:           insertResult.error ?? "no row returned"
  304:         }`,
  305:       );
  306:       continue;
  307:     }
  308: 
  309:     derivationRowsCreated += 1;
  310:   }
  311: 
  312:   return {
  313:     ok: errors.length === 0,
  314:     derivationRunId,
  315:     derivationRowsCreated,
  316:     candidateCount: candidates.length,
  317:     resolvedCandidateCount: countResolved(candidates),
  318:     unresolvedCandidateCount: countUnresolved(candidates),
  319:     warnings,
  320:     errors,
  321:     runInsert,
  322:     derivationInserts,
  323:   };
  324: }
  325: 
  326: export const categoryDerivationPersistence = {
  327:   persistCategoryDerivations,
  328: };
